{"parentUuid":"254b5af8-e07a-46e8-baea-7e2090269fde","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWt2ZbX3fsuZinw1cUxG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Post**, **Event announcement**, **Announcements** and **Page**. The event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting are listed as out of scope for this version.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q2** - **How do photos from past activities get onto the site?**\n\nThe brief wants students to \"see photos from past activities\". It also says photos should only be published when ASA has permission to use them. The seven required capabilities include no photo upload or gallery, and the manual excludes page builders and WYSIWYG editors. Three options:\n\n- **(a)** No photo feature. Photos are image files that get added to the site's files. A Post or Page shows them with a normal Markdown image reference. A \"Photos\" Page (or a Post per activity) is where they appear.\n- **(b)** A photo upload form in the admin console. Photos are stored and published like content.\n- **(c)** Skip photos in this version and mention them under \"later\".\n\n➡️ I recommend **(a)**. It needs no new data model or upload security surface. Both the sanitizer and the relative-path rule still apply to the image links. Permission stays a person's decision: a photo is only added to a Post or Page once an ASA leader confirms ASA may use it. That is a working rule for the client, not something the software checks.\n\nI don't know who at ASA grants photo permission, so I won't write that down as fact. Tell me if you know."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":765,"cache_read_input_tokens":64653,"output_tokens":454,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":765,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":454,"cache_read_input_tokens":64653,"cache_creation_input_tokens":765,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":765},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWt2Z7VMAkVj6efCXfVk","type":"assistant","uuid":"60bf4c60-2346-4789-8885-98394c74fa37","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:41:51.987Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":14,"oldLines":6,"newStart":14,"newLines":17,"lines":[" **Page**"," Permanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. {"parentUuid":"2a8b0d06-af48-44ef-b0a9-6b6f274b639a","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWt7vbxFstSQBN2AStGe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Photo**, **Photos page** and **Photo permission**. The last one is written as a working rule for this project, not as an ASA policy.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q3** - **Can an Editor change Pages, including the Photos page?**\n\nThe brief says an ASA leader manages accounts and permanent pages, and another member writes and updates event posts. That points to Pages being admin-only. You also want editors to be able to update the Photos page if the permissions allow it. Those two goals pull against each other, so the options are:\n\n- **(a)** Pages are **admin-only**. Editors can see them in the list but can't change them. The Photos page is updated by the admin.\n- **(b)** Editors can **edit existing Pages** but can't create, delete or unpublish them. The admin controls which Pages exist and are live.\n- **(c)** Editors have the same rights on Pages as on Posts.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(b)**. Editors can keep the Photos page current, and the ASA leader controls which permanent pages exist. A wrong edit by an editor can be undone, but a deleted \"About\" page would break the navigation.\n\nOne thing to flag from your field notes: you never got to test Editor permissions in WordPress, because the generated password was lost. So there's no observed behavior to lean on here, and the choice is a design decision."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":858,"cache_read_input_tokens":66036,"output_tokens":442,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":858,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":442,"cache_read_input_tokens":66036,"cache_creation_input_tokens":858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":858},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWt7v6BwjDJLqvnaeV7N","type":"assistant","uuid":"a22c60b2-1bda-47af-a2d0-e3280c794085","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:43:04.537Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":23,"oldLines":6,"newStart":23,"newLines":14,"lines":[" **Photo permission**"," A working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).","+","+**Editor**","+Another ASA member. {"parentUuid":"6bc2f117-dee2-4775-9eb4-c82989879320","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_014Ng6XhcwKJRkGUJNfaArqw","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"3c6b2554-689e-4e3a-87e2-c400030a4b89","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:44:26.447Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"3c6b2554-689e-4e3a-87e2-c400030a4b89","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWtDyRXhyBmzzJuNZqtp","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Admin** and **Editor**, with the Page rules you gave.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q4** - **What can an Editor do with Posts?**\n\nYou want event announcements to be \"quick to publish\", and the brief says an editor writes and updates event posts. The open points are whether an editor publishes directly, whose Posts an editor may touch, and whether an editor may delete:\n\n- **(a)** Editors create Posts and **publish them directly**. They can edit any Post, including ones another editor wrote. Only the admin can delete a Post.\n- **(b)** Editors create and publish Posts and edit **only their own**. Only the admin can edit others' Posts or delete any Post.\n- **(c)** Editors create Posts as drafts only. The admin has to approve and publish every Post.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(a)**. Option (c) would slow down quick announcements, and the brief describes a small group where an editor can be trusted to publish. Editing other people's Posts helps when an event's details change and the original author is away. Keeping delete for the admin protects against an accidental loss.\n\nYour field notes showed the public site listing the author as \"admin\", so I'll ask about author display in a later question."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":484,"cache_read_input_tokens":67464,"output_tokens":399,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":484,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":399,"cache_read_input_tokens":67464,"cache_creation_input_tokens":484,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":484},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWtDy1UQygvJ3YLUXd7D","type":"assistant","uuid":"8bb14359-4687-46a1-a4d5-1af98d43040a","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:44:26.447Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Writes and updates Posts. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":29,"oldLines":7,"newStart":29,"newLines":7,"lines":[" An ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links)."," "," **Editor**","-Another ASA member. Writes and updates Posts. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.","+Another ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress."," "," ## Later (out of scope for this version)"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3152ed48-344c-4188-abc8-fab1f0325c54","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2d7d4873-c513-44f1-b5fa-8cbe974e239a","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_018xgCHh11yNb2rcMtg4Qm6b","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"a7426800-63cd-484c-921c-c047e31337c7","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:45:48.469Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"a7426800-63cd-484c-921c-c047e31337c7","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWtL5ECW1ZutkrrNMJpq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Updated the **Editor** entry in `CONTEXT.md`: editors publish directly, edit any Post, and can't delete.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q5** - **Does the public site show who wrote a Post?**\n\nEach Post records an author. In WordPress your published test post showed \"admin\" as the author. On a public student site, a byline could show a real member's name or it could be left off. The options:\n\n- **(a)** Show no author on public Posts. The author is still recorded in the admin console.\n- **(b)** Show the author's display name, which the admin sets when creating the account. It could be a real name, a nickname or a role such as \"ASA Events Team\".\n- **(c)** Show every author's real full name.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(b)**. The admin decides what name appears, so no member's name is published without their agreement. The post still reads as coming from ASA. I don't know whether ASA members want their names public, so this avoids assuming they do."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":378,"cache_read_input_tokens":68463,"output_tokens":301,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":378,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":301,"cache_read_input_tokens":68463,"cache_creation_input_tokens":378,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":378},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWtL4q8ac84EcC3Jzbgu","type":"assistant","uuid":"5d5c8f09-09c9-4152-875d-2d636f1e4254","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:45:48.469Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. {"parentUuid":"4b096502-eb51-4980-bde0-51ba5e221017","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_01VF3NJ5zVzdGMJTBs1G3chv","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"dd4f1de5-0de1-4e0f-956f-b465571212f0","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:47:10.858Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"dd4f1de5-0de1-4e0f-956f-b465571212f0","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWtS8Ku5bTt8rum45zYw","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Author**, **Display name** and **Username**.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q6** - **What happens to a deactivated account's Posts and byline?**\n\nThe admin can deactivate a user, and that user then can't log in. The person may leave ASA after graduating, for example. Their Posts stay in the database with them as author. Two things need settling:\n\n- **(a)** Their published Posts **stay public** with the same display name. Deactivation only blocks login.\n- **(b)** Their published Posts stay public, but the admin can **change the display name** on the deactivated account, for example to \"ASA Events Team\", if the person wants their name off the site.\n- **(c)** Deactivating an account also unpublishes its Posts.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(b)**. It's the same mechanism as Q5, so there's no new feature. It also handles a member who wants a name removed after leaving. Option (c) would delete announcements that ASA still wants to keep, which seems wrong for a club whose members rotate.\n\nDeleting user accounts isn't among the required capabilities, so I'm assuming accounts are only deactivated. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":29,"oldLines":6,"newStart":29,"newLines":9,"lines":[" **Display name**"," The public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline."," ","+**Deactivated account**","+An account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.","+"," **Username**"," The private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default."," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a6ead6bc-f456-4445-b036-34d982310da5","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"8dac4f58-8f77-463d-a84b-1291ece42524","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_01MBRxm5wswPkaD8dj44rPHL","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"162538b2-86be-48bc-83ba-7a404f87633b","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:47:57.990Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"162538b2-86be-48bc-83ba-7a404f87633b","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWtVVANGNsvLfBivuTgH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Deactivated account**. Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q7** - **What does \"publish\" mean for ASA, given the local admin?**\n\nUnder ADR-001 the admin console runs only on a laptop, and nothing is public until `cms publish` and `cms deploy` run. That conflicts with wanting announcements to be \"quick to publish\" and with an editor publishing directly (Q4). Whoever writes a Post has to get it onto the public site somehow. Options:\n\n- **(a)** Marking a Post **Published** in the console only makes it eligible. A separate step, `cms publish` and then `cms deploy`, makes it live. Someone with the project on their laptop runs that step. Editors write and mark Posts published, and the person running the laptop (probably the admin) does the last step.\n- **(b)** Same as (a), but I document a short checklist for \"publishing an announcement\" so the person running it can do it in a couple of minutes.\n- **(c)** Change the architecture so the admin console is hosted online. That would reverse ADR-001.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(b)**. ADR-001 is set by the assignment, so (c) is out. Since editors can't run the laptop step themselves, I'd word it this way in the glossary. **Published** is a status. **Live** means the static site has been rebuilt and deployed. An editor's \"quick\" publishing means marking a Post Published. The announcement isn't visible to visitors until someone runs the deploy.\n\nWho actually holds the laptop and runs the deploy for ASA? I don't know, and I won't assume it's the admin. Please tell me."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":333,"cache_read_input_tokens":70492,"output_tokens":548,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":333,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":548,"cache_read_input_tokens":70492,"cache_creation_input_tokens":333,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":333},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWtVUgbK4mGttJxK5Hew","type":"assistant","uuid":"b30cf945-b9e1-4751-aa32-f2599ebfe941","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:47:57.989Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"b30991a2-3f3e-4176-b027-5188652fbbb4","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:49:15.901Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","oldString":"## Roles\n","newString":"## Publishing\n\n**Published**\nA status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.\n\n**Live**\nVisible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.\n\n**Release checklist**\nA short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.\n\n## Roles\n","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Deactivated account**\nAn account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":35,"oldLines":6,"newStart":35,"newLines":17,"lines":[" **Username**"," The private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default."," ","+## Publishing","+","+**Published**","+A status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.","+","+**Live**","+Visible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.","+","+**Release checklist**","+A short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.","+"," ## Roles"," "," **Admin**"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"507d3a45-f76f-4243-9f64-ae8909362e46","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"fb1ef664-8a5f-4f09-9ef1-23cdedf24342","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWtbaZiq6Av5GvFXWt6p","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Published**, **Live** and **Release checklist**. The demo owner is you, and no ASA deploy owner is assumed.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q8** - **What is in the public navigation, and what is on the home page?**\n\nPublished Pages appear in the navigation automatically (capability 4). The brief says information about ASA and its resources \"should remain easy to find in the navigation\", and the site should feel welcoming to someone discovering ASA for the first time. Two things to settle:\n\n1. **Where the Announcements list lives.** Is it the home page, or a separate item in the navigation?\n2. **Which Pages exist at the start.** I can't assume these are ASA's real pages. These are placeholders for the demo.\n\nProposal:\n\n- The **home page** is the Announcements list, with a short welcome line at the top.\n- The navigation has **Announcements** plus every published Page.\n- The starter Pages are **About ASA**, **Resources** and **Photos**.\n\n➡️ I recommend this proposal. A first-time visitor sees the newest announcements at once, and About sits one click away in the navigation. The welcome text stays generic. I won't write claims about ASA's history, size or programs, since I don't know them. The one line of welcome copy that we can state safely comes from your brief: a student group for African students and students with African backgrounds, welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community.\n\nIs the welcome line taken from the brief acceptable, or do you want to supply the wording? Also, are About, Resources and Photos the right starter Pages for the demo?"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":421,"cache_read_input_tokens":71508,"output_tokens":516,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":421,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":516,"cache_read_input_tokens":71508,"cache_creation_input_tokens":421,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":421},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWtbaB9u6RwEKrTgjx7s","type":"assistant","uuid":"1de6bfe0-6bab-4045-a99f-4fd9c20ba4bc","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:49:20.317Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"307ea052-374e-4a51-9edd-4a79c729031e","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:54:36.658Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","oldString":"## Publishing\n","newString":"## Public site\n\n**Home page**\nA short Welcome text followed by the latest Announcements.\n\n**Welcome text**\nThe short greeting at the top of the Home page. It uses the wording from `notes/client-brief.md`: ASA is a Kenyon student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, while welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community. No other claims about ASA are made.\n\n**Navigation**\nThe public menu: Announcements plus every published Page. The starter Pages for the demo are About ASA, Resources and Photos. They are placeholders, not a claim about ASA's real pages.\n\n**Event card**\nHow an Announcement appears in the Announcements list. It is clear and readable on a phone.\n\n**Decorative graphic**\nA licensed image or graphic used for looks only. It is never presented as a photo of a real ASA event. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then.\n\n## Publishing\n","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Deactivated account**\nAn account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Published**\nA status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.\n\n**Live**\nVisible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.\n\n**Release checklist**\nA short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":35,"oldLines":6,"newStart":35,"newLines":23,"lines":[" **Username**"," The private name an account signs in with. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then.","+"," ## Publishing"," "," **Published**"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c4fd1000-fe8f-4323-b8fd-697e1dd426a0","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"836f6c3e-17d0-41bc-a5f1-096e599f66ad","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWu1DwueTpJCNNPRMj3R","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Home page**, **Welcome text**, **Navigation**, **Event card** and **Decorative graphic**.\n\nDesign goals like \"polished\" and \"works well on a phone\" aren't glossary terms, so I've left them out of `CONTEXT.md`. They'll go into the spec at `/to-spec`.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q9** - **How does an Editor deal with slugs on Posts?**\n\nA slug is the URL-safe version of a title, such as `afrobeat-night`. Q3 made Page slugs admin-only. For Posts, an editor who is in a hurry to publish shouldn't have to think about slugs at all. Editing a slug after a Post is live also breaks any link already shared. Options:\n\n- **(a)** The slug is **generated from the title** when the Post is first saved. The editor never has to fill it in. It stays fixed after that, even if the title is corrected. Only the admin can change it.\n- **(b)** Same generation, but the editor can change the slug at any time.\n- **(c)** The slug is a required field the editor types.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(a)**. It keeps the \"no complicated form\" goal from Q1. It also handles the case you raised in Q4, where an editor fixes a time or location, or even a title typo, without breaking a link that's already been shared. If two Posts would get the same title, the second gets a numbered ending so URLs stay unique.\n\nI'll leave the exact URL shape (for example, whether a date appears in it) for the spec. That's a technical choice, and your WordPress notes only show that date-based URLs exist, not that ASA needs one."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":901,"cache_read_input_tokens":72628,"output_tokens":537,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":901,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":537,"cache_read_input_tokens":72628,"cache_creation_input_tokens":901,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":901},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWu1DZawhtmF1ou7DZrJ","type":"assistant","uuid":"3d4b8eb3-f833-485b-abb6-8b6d8a4fe57f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:54:41.463Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

A Post's slug is generated from its title on the first save, with a number added if it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or event details are corrected, so shared links keep working. Only the Admin may change a slug, and is warned that this can break an old link. A Page's slug is also Admin-only.\n\n**Event announcement**","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Deactivated account**\nAn account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Public site\n\n**Home page**\nA short Welcome text followed by the latest Announcements.\n\n**Welcome text**\nThe short greeting at the top of the Home page. It uses the wording from `notes/client-brief.md`: ASA is a Kenyon student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, while welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community. No other claims about ASA are made.\n\n**Navigation**\nThe public menu: Announcements plus every published Page. The starter Pages for the demo are About ASA, Resources and Photos. They are placeholders, not a claim about ASA's real pages.\n\n**Event card**\nHow an Announcement appears in the Announcements list. It is clear and readable on a phone.\n\n**Decorative graphic**\nA licensed image or graphic used for looks only. It is never presented as a photo of a real ASA event. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then.\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Published**\nA status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.\n\n**Live**\nVisible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.\n\n**Release checklist**\nA short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":5,"oldLines":6,"newStart":5,"newLines":9,"lines":[" **Post**"," A dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly."," ","+**Slug**","+The URL-safe form of a title, such as `afrobeat-night`. A Post's slug is generated from its title on the first save, with a number added if it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or event details are corrected, so shared links keep working. Only the Admin may change a slug, and is warned that this can break an old link. A Page's slug is also Admin-only.","+"," **Event announcement**"," A Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content."," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"398b234a-be4d-4b30-926a-35c995402a67","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"657e3da9-a0ac-453c-a270-ace4b9d6a210","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_016G11AD2ay7MYqQZm2gtGjL","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"2233fdd7-0ffc-43c0-8067-5f24f3a730f4","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:56:17.670Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"2233fdd7-0ffc-43c0-8067-5f24f3a730f4","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWu8MeCz3M9Mq8we1g1r","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Slug**.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q10** - **What must an Editor be unable to do, and what does an Editor see when refused?**\n\nThe manual asks for a screenshot of \"the page an editor sees when refused an admin screen\", and the brief needs a \"must not happen\" list. From your answers so far, an Editor must not:\n\n- delete a Post or a Page\n- create, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page\n- change a Post's slug\n- open user management, or create, deactivate or change the role of any account\n- change any account's Display name\n\nThe open point is what happens when an Editor reaches one of these anyway, for example by typing the address directly.\n\n- **(a)** The buttons and menu items an Editor can't use are **hidden**, and the server also **refuses** the request with a plain \"You don't have permission\" page that links back to the dashboard. Hiding alone is not the protection; the refusal is.\n- **(b)** The server refuses, but the buttons stay visible, greyed out.\n- **(c)** The server silently sends the Editor back to the dashboard with no message.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(a)**. It keeps the editor's screen simple, which matches your \"no complicated form\" goal, and the enforcement doesn't depend on what the page shows. The permission page also gives us the screenshot the manual asks for, and gives a member who is refused a clear reason.\n\nIs that list complete, or is there something else an Editor must never be able to do?"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":360,"cache_read_input_tokens":74193,"output_tokens":483,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":360,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":483,"cache_read_input_tokens":74193,"cache_creation_input_tokens":360,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":360},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWu8MABvdfMynd4y76YQ","type":"assistant","uuid":"3b006481-653c-4a78-a1bd-3fbf88a4c1a2","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:56:17.670Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"738fa803-5625-45d2-a1ac-22cda80df0dd","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:57:38.694Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","oldString":"## Later (out of scope for this version)","newString":"**Permission refusal**\nWhat an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Slug**\nThe URL-safe form of a title, such as `afrobeat-night`. A Post's slug is generated from its title on the first save, with a number added if it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or event details are corrected, so shared links keep working. Only the Admin may change a slug, and is warned that this can break an old link. A Page's slug is also Admin-only.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Deactivated account**\nAn account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Public site\n\n**Home page**\nA short Welcome text followed by the latest Announcements.\n\n**Welcome text**\nThe short greeting at the top of the Home page. It uses the wording from `notes/client-brief.md`: ASA is a Kenyon student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, while welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community. No other claims about ASA are made.\n\n**Navigation**\nThe public menu: Announcements plus every published Page. The starter Pages for the demo are About ASA, Resources and Photos. They are placeholders, not a claim about ASA's real pages.\n\n**Event card**\nHow an Announcement appears in the Announcements list. It is clear and readable on a phone.\n\n**Decorative graphic**\nA licensed image or graphic used for looks only. It is never presented as a photo of a real ASA event. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then.\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Published**\nA status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.\n\n**Live**\nVisible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.\n\n**Release checklist**\nA short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":74,"oldLines":6,"newStart":74,"newLines":9,"lines":[" **Editor**"," Another ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress."," ","+**Permission refusal**","+What an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs.","+"," ## Later (out of scope for this version)"," "," - A photo upload form or gallery."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c8b59bd1-2443-4947-b748-2a9228156252","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"2be2fd32-2aa8-4557-9929-e70dc7ac8d04","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_01WgEqKsWaENmm5iXYr2hMMy","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"89370c30-3323-4365-8c4c-fbe347d06026","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:57:42.544Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"89370c30-3323-4365-8c4c-fbe347d06026","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWuEeE2H6czx8FY2FQKW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Permission refusal**.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q11** - **How does a member recover a lost password?**\n\nYour field notes say you lost the generated Editor password in WordPress before you could log in, so this is something you've already run into. This admin console runs on a laptop with no email service, so a \"forgot password\" email link isn't possible. Options:\n\n- **(a)** The **Admin sets a new password** for the account. The account owner is told the new password in person or through a channel the two of them trust. There's no self-service reset.\n- **(b)** Same as (a), and the account owner **must change the temporary password on first login**. That adds a \"change my password\" screen, which isn't one of the seven capabilities.\n- **(c)** No recovery. The Admin makes a new account.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(a)**, which keeps the scope to what the manual requires. Passwords are still stored hashed and never shown or logged. The Admin sets or resets one but can't read an existing one. If the reset password is a temporary one, it goes to the account owner outside the site.\n\nI'd note (b) under \"Later\". Its main benefit is that the Admin wouldn't know the password an Editor keeps using. Do you want (b) now, or is (a) enough for this version?"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":701,"cache_read_input_tokens":75220,"output_tokens":413,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":701,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":413,"cache_read_input_tokens":75220,"cache_creation_input_tokens":701,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":701},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWuEdqCpMpLrxPa9WKPm","type":"assistant","uuid":"44aaa26c-64b9-4bd2-a25a-8dd413229433","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:57:42.544Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"7d564315-4979-4d73-b7e9-d3d7907d23e9","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"d6934e77-2e6e-469b-9ba8-2249e6b3b459","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013E7PbYr1nTLqaChvk6tAvM","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md has been updated successfully. (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"15b9f460-7e6f-41e0-8a99-494d6fb9af95","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:58:30.325Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","oldString":"## Later (out of scope for this version)\n","newString":"**Password reset**\nThe only way to recover a lost password. The Admin sets a new password for an existing account and cannot view the old one. The new password is stored hashed and is never printed or logged. The Admin passes it to the account owner outside the CMS through a trusted channel. There is no email link and no self-service reset.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A first-login password change flow.\n","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Slug**\nThe URL-safe form of a title, such as `afrobeat-night`. A Post's slug is generated from its title on the first save, with a number added if it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or event details are corrected, so shared links keep working. Only the Admin may change a slug, and is warned that this can break an old link. A Page's slug is also Admin-only.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Deactivated account**\nAn account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Public site\n\n**Home page**\nA short Welcome text followed by the latest Announcements.\n\n**Welcome text**\nThe short greeting at the top of the Home page. It uses the wording from `notes/client-brief.md`: ASA is a Kenyon student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, while welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community. No other claims about ASA are made.\n\n**Navigation**\nThe public menu: Announcements plus every published Page. The starter Pages for the demo are About ASA, Resources and Photos. They are placeholders, not a claim about ASA's real pages.\n\n**Event card**\nHow an Announcement appears in the Announcements list. It is clear and readable on a phone.\n\n**Decorative graphic**\nA licensed image or graphic used for looks only. It is never presented as a photo of a real ASA event. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then.\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Published**\nA status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.\n\n**Live**\nVisible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.\n\n**Release checklist**\nA short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n**Permission refusal**\nWhat an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":77,"oldLines":8,"newStart":77,"newLines":13,"lines":[" **Permission refusal**"," What an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs."," ","+**Password reset**","+The only way to recover a lost password. The Admin sets a new password for an existing account and cannot view the old one. The new password is stored hashed and is never printed or logged. The Admin passes it to the account owner outside the CMS through a trusted channel. There is no email link and no self-service reset.","+"," ## Later (out of scope for this version)"," ","+- A first-login password change flow.","+"," - A photo upload form or gallery."," "," - An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting."]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7d564315-4979-4d73-b7e9-d3d7907d23e9","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"5ac9f43f-8192-465b-b265-7dcee2c45a2b","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_013E7PbYr1nTLqaChvk6tAvM","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"39fc2177-3bb7-4bbe-a075-703b643a7af8","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:58:34.098Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"39fc2177-3bb7-4bbe-a075-703b643a7af8","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWuJSzxB6RKtLheuow5N","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Password reset**, plus a first-login password change under **Later**.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q12** - **What does the Dashboard show?**\n\nCapability 5 says the dashboard has \"counts\", a content list you can filter, and an editor with a preview. Your WordPress notes liked being able to see status without opening each page: the Pages list had separate Published and Draft filters. Proposal for what each role sees on the dashboard:\n\n- **Counts:** Published Posts, Draft Posts, Published Pages and Draft Pages.\n- **Admin only:** the number of active accounts and the number of Deactivated accounts.\n- **Content list:** one list for both Posts and Pages, filtered by type (Post or Page) and by status (Draft or Published). Each row shows title, status, Display name and last-changed time.\n- **One extra signal:** a \"Changes not live yet\" note when something is Published in the console but hasn't been made Live. This follows from Q7. I'm not sure the software can tell whether the last deploy happened. If it can't, I'd drop this.\n\n➡️ I recommend the counts, the account numbers for the Admin, and the filtered list. I'd only keep the \"not live yet\" note if it's simple to do reliably.\n\nIs that the right set, or should the dashboard be simpler or show something else?"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":356,"cache_read_input_tokens":76450,"output_tokens":438,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":356,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":438,"cache_read_input_tokens":76450,"cache_creation_input_tokens":356,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":356},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWuJSbPH6Sfd33V4tP2x","type":"assistant","uuid":"fcb728e4-4b7a-41fe-a48e-9e476421083e","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:58:34.097Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
