(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"8f5b055a-775e-4b3e-ad35-3d8005de54ba","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:59:47.266Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","oldString":"## Publishing\n","newString":"## Admin console\n\n**Dashboard**\nThe first screen after signing in. It shows four content counts: Published Posts, Draft Posts, Published Pages and Draft Pages. Only the Admin also sees the number of active accounts and Deactivated accounts. It carries a clear reminder that marking content Published does not make it Live until `cms publish` and `cms deploy` run. It has no \"Changes not live yet\" indicator unless the app can determine that reliably. It stays simple on a phone.\n\n**Content list**\nOne list of Posts and Pages, filtered by type (Post or Page) and by status (Draft or Published). Each row shows title, status, Display name and last-updated time.\n\n## Publishing\n","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Slug**\nThe URL-safe form of a title, such as `afrobeat-night`. A Post's slug is generated from its title on the first save, with a number added if it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or event details are corrected, so shared links keep working. Only the Admin may change a slug, and is warned that this can break an old link. A Page's slug is also Admin-only.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Deactivated account**\nAn account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Public site\n\n**Home page**\nA short Welcome text followed by the latest Announcements.\n\n**Welcome text**\nThe short greeting at the top of the Home page. It uses the wording from `notes/client-brief.md`: ASA is a Kenyon student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, while welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community. No other claims about ASA are made.\n\n**Navigation**\nThe public menu: Announcements plus every published Page. The starter Pages for the demo are About ASA, Resources and Photos. They are placeholders, not a claim about ASA's real pages.\n\n**Event card**\nHow an Announcement appears in the Announcements list. It is clear and readable on a phone.\n\n**Decorative graphic**\nA licensed image or graphic used for looks only. It is never presented as a photo of a real ASA event. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then.\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Published**\nA status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.\n\n**Live**\nVisible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.\n\n**Release checklist**\nA short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n**Permission refusal**\nWhat an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs.\n\n**Password reset**\nThe only way to recover a lost password. The Admin sets a new password for an existing account and cannot view the old one. The new password is stored hashed and is never printed or logged. The Admin passes it to the account owner outside the CMS through a trusted channel. There is no email link and no self-service reset.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A first-login password change flow.\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":55,"oldLines":6,"newStart":55,"newLines":14,"lines":[" **Decorative graphic**"," A licensed image or graphic used for looks only. It is never presented as a photo of a real ASA event. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then."," ","+## Admin console","+","+**Dashboard**","+The first screen after signing in. It shows four content counts: Published Posts, Draft Posts, Published Pages and Draft Pages. Only the Admin also sees the number of active accounts and Deactivated accounts. It carries a clear reminder that marking content Published does not make it Live until `cms publish` and `cms deploy` run. It has no \"Changes not live yet\" indicator unless the app can determine that reliably. It stays simple on a phone.","+","+**Content list**","+One list of Posts and Pages, filtered by type (Post or Page) and by status (Draft or Published). Each row shows title, status, Display name and last-updated time.","+"," ## Publishing"," "," **Published**"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f06d786b-c5eb-45cc-98c1-38ebb8c2601a","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"be46c9c9-0d68-48a8-b6b8-fc7e0127d1ab","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"deferred_tools_record","entries":[],"toolInputCopies":[{"id":"toolu_013go3o6tAVudjKjyzAfKukx","copy":"wire"}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"84de1ef7-3068-4e11-8898-587f56c6ba79","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:59:52.049Z","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"84de1ef7-3068-4e11-8898-587f56c6ba79","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWuQ8NwdVTx19M5eyutt","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Dashboard** and **Content list**.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q13** - **How does an image file get onto the site?**\n\nQ2 settled that photos appear inside Posts and Pages through normal Markdown image references, with no upload form. That leaves a gap. `site/` is generated and never edited by hand, and the admin console has no upload feature. So an image file must be added somewhere else before a Post or Page can refer to it. That step needs access to the project's files, not just a login. An Editor who can update the Photos page body (Q3) still couldn't add a new image on their own.\n\nOptions:\n\n- **(a)** Image files are **added to the project by whoever runs the release** (you, for the demo) and then copied into `site/` by `cms publish`. Editors write the alt text and Markdown reference. The person adding the file confirms Photo permission first, which fits Q2.\n- **(b)** Editors may only **link to images hosted elsewhere**. This is simple, but ASA would depend on outside sites, and the site's rules on relative paths and Photo permission become hard to check.\n- **(c)** Build a small upload feature. This reverses Q2.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(a)**. It matches what you already decided, and it makes the release step the natural checkpoint for Photo permission. The cost is that an Editor can't add a photo alone. The Photos page body can be kept current, but a new photo needs the release-holder. I'd record this plainly so the client isn't surprised.\n\nDo you accept that limit for this version?"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":850,"cache_read_input_tokens":77412,"output_tokens":505,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":850,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":505,"cache_read_input_tokens":77412,"cache_creation_input_tokens":850,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":850},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWuQ7ydHrJWaXQvyqJuY","type":"assistant","uuid":"186b21a0-b6b2-4ae8-8809-c271fabd3932","timestamp":"2026-09-29T01:59:52.049Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"f3631fc2-eeed-4ceb-9633-437f1764cacc","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWuUjcrc3qp6LbyNroia","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Both edits are in `CONTEXT.md`. The **Photo** entry now records the limit that an Editor can't add an image file alone. The **Release checklist** entry now names that limit and says Photo permission is confirmed there.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q14** - **What Markdown can Posts and Pages use, and what does the preview show?**\n\nThe rules say user-written Markdown is sanitized before it is rendered anywhere. Editors are non-technical, and Q1 asked for event details to sit clearly in the body of a Post. Proposal:\n\n- **Allowed:** headings, bold and italic, bullet and numbered lists, links, images (with alt text), and simple line breaks.\n- **Not allowed:** raw HTML, scripts, embedded frames and forms. Anything like that is stripped rather than shown.\n- **Preview:** the editor's preview shows exactly what the sanitized public version will look like, including how the Event card appears.\n- **Links:** external links are allowed, and open normally. The Markdown reference for an image uses a relative path.\n- **Suggested body outline** for an Event announcement: What, When, Where, Who can attend. This would be a hint shown on the new Post screen, not a required form. It supports Q1's \"no complicated form\" while making the details easy to find.\n\n➡️ I recommend all of this. {"parentUuid":"ee5fdeae-6c9d-4e19-be57-bc7cbb31c03b","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"8a986ba9-7e44-4072-a522-db235106d967","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BAKAF2xe7kvy4kqX3sDhVN","type":"tool_result","content":"The file /Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"106fd81a-e047-4ca2-b364-7ea5a982bdda","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:02:10.345Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","oldString":"## Publishing\n","newString":"## Writing\n\n**Body**\nThe Markdown text of a Post or Page. Allowed: headings, emphasis, lists, links, and images with alt text. Raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms are stripped and never run. Sanitizing happens in both the Preview and the public export.\n\n**Preview**\nThe editor's view of a Post or Page. It shows the same rendered, sanitized content as the public page, not a separate approximation.\n\n**Event details hint**\nA short hint near the Post editor suggesting the body cover What, When, Where and Who can attend. It is not pre-filled into the body, and an Editor can hide it without deleting any content.\n\n## Publishing\n","originalFile":"# CONTEXT.md glossary\n\n## Content\n\n**Post**\nA dated piece of writing with a title, a Markdown body, a status, an author and timestamps. Posts are for things that need to go out quickly.\n\n**Slug**\nThe URL-safe form of a title, such as `afrobeat-night`. A Post's slug is generated from its title on the first save, with a number added if it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or event details are corrected, so shared links keep working. Only the Admin may change a slug, and is warned that this can break an old link. A Page's slug is also Admin-only.\n\n**Event announcement**\nA Post that announces an event, such as the Afrobeat event. It has no event-date field of its own. The event's date, time, location and who can attend are written clearly in the body. _Avoid_: \"Event\" as a separate kind of content.\n\n**Announcements**\nThe public list of Posts, newest first. It is not \"Upcoming Events\": newest Posts are not necessarily future events. _Avoid_: \"Upcoming Events\" for this list.\n\n**Page**\nPermanent content, such as information about ASA and cultural resources. A published Page appears in the public navigation.\n\n**Photo**\nAn image shown inside a Post or Page. Photos are not uploaded through a separate feature and there is no gallery. Every photo has descriptive alt text. An Editor writes the caption, alt text and Markdown reference but cannot add a new image file alone: the release-holder adds approved image files, and publishing copies them into the public site. Demo illustrations are labeled as illustrative and have usable rights.\n\n**Photos page**\nA Page that collects selected photos from past activities.\n\n**Photo permission**\nA working rule for this project: an ASA leader checks that ASA has permission to use a real photo before it is added to a Post or Page. ASA's formal approval process, if any, is unknown and is not described here. The class demo uses placeholders or images we have permission to use.\n\n**Author**\nThe account that wrote a Post. Recorded in the admin console for every Post.\n\n**Display name**\nThe public name shown as a Post's byline, set by the Admin when creating the account. It can be a name, a nickname or a team name such as \"ASA Events Team\", so no member's real name is public without their agreement. _Avoid_: showing the **Username** as the byline.\n\n**Deactivated account**\nAn account whose owner can no longer sign in. Its published Posts stay public and the original Author relationship is kept. If a former member wants their name removed, the Admin changes the account's Display name (for example to \"ASA Events Team\"). Accounts are deactivated, never deleted.\n\n**Username**\nThe private name an account signs in with. It is never shown publicly as a byline by default.\n\n## Public site\n\n**Home page**\nA short Welcome text followed by the latest Announcements.\n\n**Welcome text**\nThe short greeting at the top of the Home page. It uses the wording from `notes/client-brief.md`: ASA is a Kenyon student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, while welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community. No other claims about ASA are made.\n\n**Navigation**\nThe public menu: Announcements plus every published Page. The starter Pages for the demo are About ASA, Resources and Photos. They are placeholders, not a claim about ASA's real pages.\n\n**Event card**\nHow an Announcement appears in the Announcements list. It is clear and readable on a phone.\n\n**Decorative graphic**\nA licensed image or graphic used for looks only. It is never presented as a photo of a real ASA event. Until ASA has permission for real photos, the Photos page says that actual event photos will be added then.\n\n## Admin console\n\n**Dashboard**\nThe first screen after signing in. It shows four content counts: Published Posts, Draft Posts, Published Pages and Draft Pages. Only the Admin also sees the number of active accounts and Deactivated accounts. It carries a clear reminder that marking content Published does not make it Live until `cms publish` and `cms deploy` run. It has no \"Changes not live yet\" indicator unless the app can determine that reliably. It stays simple on a phone.\n\n**Content list**\nOne list of Posts and Pages, filtered by type (Post or Page) and by status (Draft or Published). Each row shows title, status, Display name and last-updated time.\n\n## Publishing\n\n**Published**\nA status in the admin console meaning a Post or Page is ready for the public site. It is not yet visible to visitors. Drafts are never Published and never leave the database.\n\n**Live**\nVisible to visitors: someone has run `cms publish` and `cms deploy` after the content was marked Published. _Avoid_: saying \"published\" when you mean Live.\n\n**Release checklist**\nA short, repeatable list of steps for making Published content Live. It also states the limit that an Editor cannot add a new image file alone, and is where Photo permission is confirmed before a real photo is added. For the class demo, the project author runs it from their own laptop. ASA has no known person responsible for this step, and none is assumed.\n\n## Roles\n\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n**Permission refusal**\nWhat an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs.\n\n**Password reset**\nThe only way to recover a lost password. The Admin sets a new password for an existing account and cannot view the old one. The new password is stored hashed and is never printed or logged. The Admin passes it to the account owner outside the CMS through a trusted channel. There is no email link and no self-service reset.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A first-login password change flow.\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":63,"oldLines":6,"newStart":63,"newLines":17,"lines":[" **Content list**"," One list of Posts and Pages, filtered by type (Post or Page) and by status (Draft or Published). Each row shows title, status, Display name and last-updated time."," ","+## Writing","+","+**Body**","+The Markdown text of a Post or Page. Allowed: headings, emphasis, lists, links, and images with alt text. Raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms are stripped and never run. Sanitizing happens in both the Preview and the public export.","+","+**Preview**","+The editor's view of a Post or Page. It shows the same rendered, sanitized content as the public page, not a separate approximation.","+","+**Event details hint**","+A short hint near the Post editor suggesting the body cover What, When, Where and Who can attend. It is not pre-filled into the body, and an Editor can hide it without deleting any content.","+"," ## Publishing"," "," **Published**"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false,"contentNotInModelContext":true},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ee5fdeae-6c9d-4e19-be57-bc7cbb31c03b","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"d41a1861-54e9-4004-ae5b-ba6f65ba20ad","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWuafhdmY6dHsCqQk7WG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **Body**, **Preview** and **Event details hint**.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q15** - **How are Announcements ordered and dated?**\n\nEvent details often change close to the event (Q4). If the list is ordered and dated by the last edit, a small fix would move an old Announcement back to the top, so the \"newest first\" order would stop meaning anything. The options:\n\n- **(a)** Order by the **date first Published**, newest first. Each Event card shows that date. If a Post is edited after it went out, the card also shows \"Updated <date>\", so visitors can tell the details changed.\n- **(b)** Order by last-edited date. A correction moves the Post to the top.\n- **(c)** Order by the date first Published and never show that a Post was edited.\n\nI'd also settle how many show where:\n\n- **Home page:** the 5 most recent Announcements, with a link to all of them.\n- **Announcements page:** all Published Posts, newest first.\n\n➡️ I recommend **(a)** with those counts. A visitor checking an event needs to know if the time was corrected, and (a) shows that without reordering the list. The number 5 is a guess for a short home page. Change it if you want a different number.\n\nOne case to decide: if a Post is unpublished and later Published again, does it keep its original date, or count as new? My suggestion is that it keeps the original date."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":682,"cache_read_input_tokens":80734,"output_tokens":444,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":682,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":444,"cache_read_input_tokens":80734,"cache_creation_input_tokens":682,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":682},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWuaf8Pn3urQs2d4vNJZ","type":"assistant","uuid":"76629baf-c832-4e2a-af69-621abfb4341f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:02:14.710Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"56f2e6e7-8c59-4b9d-88f8-6d21481d4a3b","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWueVFSA88AFHdYo7nwz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: **First-published date** and **Updated date**. **Announcements** now says how the list is ordered and that the Home page shows five.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q16** - **What is explicitly out of scope for this version?**\n\nThe manual says to stop the grill once you can say what is out of scope. It excludes multi-site, drag-and-drop page builders and rich-text WYSIWYG editors. Your answers so far have added more. My proposed **Out of scope** list:\n\n- A separate Event content type, an event-date field, and automatic upcoming/past sorting (Q1)\n- A photo upload form or gallery (Q2, Q13)\n- Account deletion, and a first-login password change (Q6, Q11)\n- Email-based password reset or any email sending (Q11)\n- A \"Changes not live yet\" indicator (Q12)\n- Public features that need a server: search, comments, sign-ups or RSVPs (ADR-001)\n- Any claim about ASA's real history, members, leaders, officers, schedule or approval process (your instruction not to invent facts)\n\nTwo items I'm not sure belong here:\n\n- **RSVP or sign-up for events.** The brief doesn't mention it, and a static site can't collect one, but ASA might expect it.\n- **Categories or tags for Announcements**, for example to separate cultural resources from events. The brief handles that split with Pages, so I'd leave tags out.\n\n➡️ I recommend that whole list, with RSVP and tags both out. Is anything missing, or is any item one you want to keep in?"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":683,"cache_read_input_tokens":82024,"output_tokens":512,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":683,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":512,"cache_read_input_tokens":82024,"cache_creation_input_tokens":683,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":683},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWueUmuxA23ZCXYhjHFb","type":"assistant","uuid":"074f8e1c-7447-4bfa-b8ac-7ad0ea3ca00b","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:03:06.174Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"8d0286d5-fad0-4129-8034-46d2827add2a","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"b6140066-19d9-448d-a869-a82984848315","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016AUS5his3SRRBs4gd4zute","type":"tool_result","content":"95\t\n96\t**Admin**\n97\tAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n98\t\n99\t**Editor**\n100\tAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n101\t\n102\t**Permission refusal**\n103\tWhat an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs.\n104\t\n105\t**Password reset**\n106\tThe only way to recover a lost password. The Admin sets a new password for an existing account and cannot view the old one. The new password is stored hashed and is never printed or logged. The Admin passes it to the account owner outside the CMS through a trusted channel. There is no email link and no self-service reset.\n107\t\n108\t## Later (out of scope for this version)\n109\t\n110\t- A first-login password change flow.\n111\t\n112\t- A photo upload form or gallery.\n113\t\n114\t- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n115\t"}]},"uuid":"d5f3ee93-1410-480c-9d2d-8f92bd69f775","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:03:33.763Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms/CONTEXT.md","content":"\n**Admin**\nAn ASA leader. Manages accounts and permanent Pages: creates and deletes Pages, publishes or unpublishes them, and changes Page titles or slugs (which affect navigation and links).\n\n**Editor**\nAnother ASA member. Creates and publishes Posts directly, because speed matters for announcements. May edit any Post, including another editor's, so event details can be corrected close to the event. May not delete a Post; only the Admin can. May update the body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, but may not create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page. This is a design choice for the ASA site, not something observed in WordPress.\n\n**Permission refusal**\nWhat an Editor sees on any attempt at an Admin-only action, whether by button, typed address or direct submission: a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. Admin-only controls are also hidden from an Editor's normal screens, but hiding is never the protection. Editors may manage Posts and update the body of existing Pages; they may not manage users, delete content, change Page structure or publication status, or change slugs.\n\n**Password reset**\nThe only way to recover a lost password. The Admin sets a new password for an existing account and cannot view the old one. The new password is stored hashed and is never printed or logged. The Admin passes it to the account owner outside the CMS through a trusted channel. There is no email link and no self-service reset.\n\n## Later (out of scope for this version)\n\n- A first-login password change flow.\n\n- A photo upload form or gallery.\n\n- An event-date field and automatic upcoming/past sorting.\n","numLines":21,"startLine":95,"totalLines":115}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8d0286d5-fad0-4129-8034-46d2827add2a","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"b74cb8eb-1016-41ac-b8ce-5fed9d0a655d","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWuh32wSnuLvqbMKcAGS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Recorded in `CONTEXT.md`: the **Later** section is now **Out of scope for this version**. It has your full list, and the photo item is clarified as you asked.\n\n---\n\n❓ **Q17** - **Is this enough to move on to `/to-spec`?**\n\nThe manual says to stop when you can answer these without hedging. Here's where each one stands:\n\n- **What is a Post?** A dated piece of writing. Event announcements are ordinary Posts, and the event details go in the Body.\n- **What is a Page?** Permanent content. Published Pages appear in the Navigation. The starter Pages are About ASA, Resources and Photos.\n- **Who may do what?** Settled in Q3, Q4, Q10 and Q11. The Admin manages accounts, Pages, slugs, deletion and publication status. Editors manage Posts and update Page bodies.\n- **What does publishing mean?** Published is a status. Live is when `cms publish` and `cms deploy` have run.\n- **What is out of scope?** The list above.\n\nTwo topics haven't been covered, and I'd like your call on each before we stop:\n\n1. **The first Admin account.** Nobody can create it through the console, because creating accounts needs an Admin. I'd propose that it's created from the command line during setup, using a password taken from the environment and not stored in the repo. That follows the security rules. This is a technical choice, so I'd leave the exact command to the spec.\n2. **Accessibility and readability.** You asked for readable text and phone-friendly layout. I'd add that images need alt text (already decided), pages need a sensible heading order, and text and buttons need enough contrast. This would go into the spec as requirements, not into the glossary.\n\n➡️ I recommend covering both in the spec without further questions, and treating the grill as finished once you confirm. I've held off on writing an ADR. None of the decisions so far are both hard to reverse and surprising, and ADR-001 already covers the big one.\n\nDo you confirm that we've reached a shared understanding? If so, the next step is `/to-spec`. {"parentUuid":"f2a18cc4-940e-4211-b673-9871205639b2","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"6dad0a2d-b6ad-4927-91d7-b6bbc8f7f9ef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /Users/yawoppong-krampah/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/mattpocock-skills/1.2.3/skills/engineering/to-spec\n\nThis skill takes the current conversation context and codebase understanding and produces a spec. Do NOT interview the user; just synthesize what you already know.\n\nThe issue tracker and triage label vocabulary should have been provided to you. If not, tell the user to run `/setup-matt-pocock-skills`.\n\n## Process\n\n1. Explore the repo to understand the current state of the codebase, if you haven't already. Use the project's domain glossary vocabulary throughout the spec, and respect any ADRs in the area you're touching.\n\n2. Sketch out the seams at which you're going to test the feature. Existing seams should be preferred to new ones. Use the highest seam possible. If new seams are needed, propose them at the highest point you can. The fewer seams across the codebase, the better - the ideal number is one.\n\nCheck with the user that these seams match their expectations.\n\n3. Write the spec using the template below, then publish it to the project issue tracker. Apply the `ready-for-agent` triage label - no need for additional triage.\n\n<spec-template>\n\n## Problem Statement\n\nThe problem that the user is facing, from the user's perspective.\n\n## Solution\n\nThe solution to the problem, from the user's perspective.\n\n## User Stories\n\nA LONG, numbered list of user stories. Each user story should be in the format of:\n\n1. As an <actor>, I want a <feature>, so that <benefit>\n\n<user-story-example>\n1. As a mobile bank customer, I want to see balance on my accounts, so that I can make better informed decisions about my spending\n</user-story-example>\n\nThis list of user stories should be extremely extensive and cover all aspects of the feature.\n\n## Implementation Decisions\n\nA list of implementation decisions that were made. This can include:\n\n- The modules that will be built/modified\n- The interfaces of those modules that will be modified\n- Technical clarifications from the developer\n- Architectural decisions\n- Schema changes\n- API contracts\n- Specific interactions\n\nDo NOT include specific file paths or code snippets. They may end up being outdated very quickly.\n\nException: if a prototype produced a snippet that encodes a decision more precisely than prose can (state machine, reducer, schema, type shape), inline it within the relevant decision and note briefly that it came from a prototype. Trim to the decision-rich parts, not a working demo, just the important bits.\n\n## Testing Decisions\n\nA list of testing decisions that were made. Include:\n\n- A description of what makes a good test (only test external behavior, not implementation details)\n- Which modules will be tested\n- Prior art for the tests (i.e. similar types of tests in the codebase)\n\n## Out of Scope\n\nA description of the things that are out of scope for this spec.\n\n## Further Notes\n\nAny further notes about the feature.\n\n</spec-template>\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"turnCompanion":true,"uuid":"3b3945fe-800f-4011-acd4-93d023f49392","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:04:28.898Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"68b25a1a-8192-4021-a2c1-9f6fadf02b6f","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"6dad0a2d-b6ad-4927-91d7-b6bbc8f7f9ef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018dU3jngfSQCVsmTK4nhFzT","type":"tool_result","content":"# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. Use the `gh` CLI for all operations.\n\n## Conventions\n\n- **Create an issue**: `gh issue create --title \"...\" --body \"...\"`. Use a heredoc for multi-line bodies.\n- **Read an issue**: `gh issue view <number> --comments`, filtering comments by `jq` and also fetching labels.\n- **List issues**: `gh issue list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,comments --jq '[.[] | {number, title, body, labels: [.labels[].name], comments: [.comments[].body]}]'` with appropriate `--label` and `--state` filters.\n- **Comment on an issue**: `gh issue comment <number> --body \"...\"`\n- **Apply / remove labels**: `gh issue edit <number> --add-label \"...\"` / `--remove-label \"...\"`\n- **Close**: `gh issue close <number> --comment \"...\"`\n\nInfer the repo from `git remote -v`; `gh` does this automatically when run inside a clone.\n\n## Pull requests as a triage surface\n\n**PRs as a request surface: no.** _(Set to `yes` if this repo treats external PRs as feature requests; `/triage` reads this flag.)_\n\nWhen set to `yes`, PRs run through the same labels and states as issues, using the `gh pr` equivalents:\n\n- **Read a PR**: `gh pr view <number> --comments` and `gh pr diff <number>` for the diff.\n- **List external PRs for triage**: `gh pr list --state open --json number,title,body,labels,author,authorAssociation,comments` then keep only `authorAssociation` of `CONTRIBUTOR`, `FIRST_TIME_CONTRIBUTOR`, or `NONE` (drop `OWNER`/`MEMBER`/`COLLABORATOR`).\n- **Comment / label / close**: `gh pr comment`, `gh pr edit --add-label`/`--remove-label`, `gh pr close`.\n\nGitHub shares one number space across issues and PRs, so a bare `#42` may be either: resolve with `gh pr view 42` and fall back to `gh issue view 42`.\n\n## When a skill says \"publish to the issue tracker\"\n\nCreate a GitHub issue.\n\n## When a skill says \"fetch the relevant ticket\"\n\nRun `gh issue view <number> --comments`.\n\n## Wayfinding operations\n\nUsed by `/wayfinder`. The **map** is a single issue with **child** issues as tickets.\n\n- **Map**: a single issue labelled `wayfinder:map`, holding the Notes / Decisions-so-far / Fog body. `gh issue create --label wayfinder:map`.\n- **Child ticket**: an issue linked to the map as a GitHub sub-issue (`gh api` on the sub-issues endpoint). Where sub-issues aren't enabled, add the child to a task list in the map body and put `Part of #<map>` at the top of the child body. Labels: `wayfinder:<type>` (`research`/`prototype`/`grilling`/`task`). Once claimed, the ticket is assigned to the driving dev.\n- **Blocking**: GitHub's **native issue dependencies**, the canonical, UI-visible representation. Add an edge with `gh api --method POST repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<child>/dependencies/blocked_by -F issue_id=<blocker-db-id>`, where `<blocker-db-id>` is the blocker's numeric **database id** (`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/issues/<n> --jq .id`, _not_ the `#number` or `node_id`). GitHub reports `issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by` (open blockers only, the live gate). Where dependencies aren't available, fall back to a `Blocked by: #<n>, #<n>` line at the top of the child body. A ticket is unblocked when every blocker is closed.\n- **Frontier query**: list the map's open children (`gh issue list --state open`, scoped to the map's sub-issues / task list), drop any with an open blocker (`issue_dependencies_summary.blocked_by > 0`, or an open issue in the `Blocked by` line) or an assignee; first in map order wins.\n- **Claim**: `gh issue edit <n> --add-assignee @me`, the session's first write.\n- **Resolve**: `gh issue comment <n> --body \"<answer>\"`, then `gh issue close <n>`, then append a context pointer (gist + link) to the map's Decisions-so-far.\n----\n# ADR-002: FastAPI + Jinja + SQLite, with no frontend framework\n\n**Status:** accepted (set by the assignment)\n**Date:** 2026-09-22\n\n## Context\n\nThe project has to be readable by someone who has never built a web application,\ngradable from a clean clone, and finishable in two weeks on a $20 plan. Options\nconsidered: Django (batteries included, but a large amount of generated code to\nread), Flask (similar to FastAPI here), a JavaScript stack (a second language and\na build step), and FastAPI with server-rendered templates.\n\n## Decision\n\nFastAPI with Jinja2 templates, SQLite for storage, argon2 for password hashing,\nand an HTML sanitizer for user Markdown. No frontend framework, no build step.\n\n## Consequences\n\n- Every page is server-rendered, so \"view source\" shows what the code produced,\n  which makes debugging visible.\n- SQLite is a single file, so a grader can seed and inspect it easily. It is\n  gitignored; `scripts/seed_demo.py` rebuilds it.\n- No build step means the file you preview is the file that ships.\n- Interactive flourishes (live preview without reload, drag-and-drop) cost more\n  here than in a JavaScript stack. That is an accepted trade.\n----\n.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\n.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py\n.claude/settings.json\n.env.example\n.gitignore\nCLAUDE.md\nCONTEXT.md\nREADME.md\nSECURITY-CHECKLIST.md\napp/__init__.py\napp/cli.py\napp/main.py\napp/publish.py\napp/settings.py\ndocs/adr/ADR-001-local-admin-static-public.md\ndocs/adr/ADR-002-stack.md\ndocs/agents/domain.md\ndocs/agents/issue-tracker.md\ndocs/handoff/.gitkeep\ndocs/manual_iphs400_mp2-web-cms_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-grading-rubric_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-setup_context-threshold-hook_20260922.md\ndocs/process/.gitkeep\ndocs/screenshots/.gitkeep\ndocs/transcripts/iphs400_mp2-cms_chat-session_01_yaw-oppong-krampah_20260928.md\nnotes/.gitkeep\nnotes/cms-field-notes.md\nnotes/setup_check.md\nnotes/token-budget-plan.md\nnotes/usage-ledger.csv\npyproject.toml\nscripts/check_submission.py\nscripts/seed_demo.py\nscripts/usage_report.py\ntemplates/admin/hello.html\ntemplates/base.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\ntests/conftest.py\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py\ntests/test_t00.py\ntests/test_usage_report.py\nuv.lock\n----\n[project]\nname = \"iphs400-mp2-cms\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\ndescription = \"IPHS 400 MP2 — a small web CMS with a local admin console and a static public site\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\ndependencies = [\n    \"fastapi>=0.115\",\n    \"uvicorn[standard]>=0.32\",\n    \"jinja2>=3.1\",\n    \"python-multipart>=0.0.12\",   # form posts\n    \"itsdangerous>=2.2\",          # signed session cookies\n    \"argon2-cffi>=23.1\",          # password hashing (never store plain passwords)\n    \"markdown-it-py>=3.0\",        # Markdown -> HTML\n    \"nh3>=0.2\",                   # HTML sanitizer (strips <script> etc.)\n    \"ghp-import>=2.1\",            # push site/ to the gh-pages branch\n]\n\n[dependency-groups]\ndev = [\"pytest>=8.3\", \"httpx>=0.28\"]\n\n[project.scripts]\ncms = \"app.cli:main\"\n\n[tool.pytest.ini_options]\ntestpaths = [\"tests\"]\n\n[build-system]\nrequires = [\"hatchling\"]\nbuild-backend = \"hatchling.build\"\n\n[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]\npackages = [\"app\"]\n----\n\"\"\"Shared test fixtures.\n\n`client` gives you the app. `client_as(role)` gives you a client that is logged\nin as a seeded user of that role — it works as soon as your login route exists,\nso access-control tests stay one line:\n\n    def test_editor_cannot_manage_users(client_as):\n        assert client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/users\").status_code in (302, 403)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\nfrom fastapi.testclient import TestClient\n\nfrom app.main import create_app\n\n# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\n# tests they are fixed and meaningless.\nDEMO_USERS = {\n    \"admin\": {\"email\": \"admin@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-admin-pw\"},\n    \"editor\": {\"email\": \"editor@example.test\", \"password\": \"test-editor-pw\"},\n}\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client() -> TestClient:\n    return TestClient(create_app())\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef client_as():\n    \"\"\"Return a factory: client_as(\"editor\") -> a logged-in TestClient.\"\"\"\n\n    def _login(role: str) -> TestClient:\n        user = DEMO_USERS[role]\n        c = TestClient(create_app())\n        response = c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": user[\"email\"],\n                                          \"password\": user[\"password\"]},\n                          follow_redirects=False)\n        if response.status_code == 404:\n            pytest.skip(\"No /login route yet — build the login ticket first.\")\n        assert response.status_code in (200, 302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\naccessibility\tBarrier affecting people with disabilities\t#f143ab\nbug\tSomething isn't working\t#d73a4a\ndocumentation\tImprovements or additions to documentation\t#0075ca\nduplicate\tThis issue or pull request already exists\t#cfd3d7\nenhancement\tNew feature or request\t#a2eeef\ngood first issue\tGood for newcomers\t#7057ff\nhelp wanted\tExtra attention is needed\t#008672\ninvalid\tThis doesn't seem right\t#e4e669\nquestion\tFurther information is requested\t#d876e3\nwontfix\tThis will not be worked on\t#ffffff","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"b4190bd0-7386-434f-87d3-59d68442aa4c","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:04:32.129Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"# Issue tracker: GitHub\n\nIssues and specs for this repo live as GitHub issues. {"parentUuid":"d98db3be-3731-43d2-b916-0c9ea3a53c63","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"6dad0a2d-b6ad-4927-91d7-b6bbc8f7f9ef","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BdZn6Xo6DufrgFyP4AxCQ2","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. These pass in the fresh template.\n\nDo not delete them. If a change breaks them, the change broke the app's front\ndoor.\n\"\"\"","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"63776c89-2be1-42d6-acf4-77903c73ce8f","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:04:34.744Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"\"\"\"The FastAPI application.\n\nT00 (already done): the admin console answers at /admin and the public site\nanswers at /. That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.templating import Jinja2Templates\n\nfrom app import settings\n\ntemplates = Jinja2Templates(directory=str(settings.TEMPLATES))\n\n\ndef create_app() -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"IPHS 400 MP2 CMS\")\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\", {\"title\": \"Admin\"}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Render the public site into site/ as plain HTML.\n\nT00 publishes a placeholder home page. As you build content types, extend\nrender_site() to write one file per published item. Two rules the rubric checks:\n\n  1. Only PUBLISHED content is written here. A draft that reaches site/ is a bug.\n  2. Every href and src is RELATIVE (\"style.css\", \"posts/x.html\"), never\n     root-absolute (\"/style.css\"), because Pages serves this from a subfolder.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport shutil\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom jinja2 import Environment, FileSystemLoader, select_autoescape\n\nfrom app import settings\n\nCSS = \"\"\"/* Minimal starter styles — make them yours. */\n:root { color-scheme: light dark; }\nbody { font: 16px/1.6 system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0 auto; max-width: 42rem; padding: 1rem; }\nheader a { font-weight: 700; text-decoration: none; }\nmain { margin-block: 2rem; }\n\"\"\"\n\n\ndef environment() -> Environment:\n    return Environment(\n        loader=FileSystemLoader(str(settings.TEMPLATES)),\n        autoescape=select_autoescape([\"html\"]),\n    )\n\n\ndef render_site(out: Path | None = None) -> Path:\n    out = out or settings.SITE\n    if out.exists():\n        shutil.rmtree(out)\n    out.mkdir(parents=True)\n    env = environment()\n    (out / \"style.css\").write_text(CSS)\n    (out / \"index.html\").write_text(\n        env.get_template(\"public/home.html\").render(\n            title=settings.SITE_TITLE, items=[], css_path=\"style.css\",\n            home_path=\"index.html\",\n        )\n    )\n    return out\n\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n\n    uv run cms serve      # admin console + public preview at http://localhost:8000\n    uv run cms publish    # render site/ from published content\n    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\n\nfrom app import settings\nfrom app.publish import render_site\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"cms\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\", required=True)\n    serve = sub.add_parser(\"serve\", help=\"run the admin console locally\")\n    serve.add_argument(\"--port\", type=int, default=8000)\n    serve.add_argument(\"--reload\", action=\"store_true\", default=True)\n    sub.add_parser(\"publish\", help=\"render site/ from published content\")\n    deploy = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n    deploy.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    if args.command == \"serve\":\n        import uvicorn\n\n        uvicorn.run(\"app.main:app\", port=args.port, reload=args.reload)\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"publish\":\n        out = render_site()\n        print(f\"Wrote {out}. Preview it with:  python3 -m http.server -d {out} 8001\")\n        return 0\n\n    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n        if not settings.SITE.exists():\n            print(\"site/ does not exist yet — run `uv run cms publish` first.\")\n            return 1\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, \"-m\", \"ghp_import\", \"-n\", \"-p\", \"-m\", args.message,\n             str(settings.SITE)],\n        )\n        if result.returncode == 0:\n            print(\"Pushed to gh-pages. Settings -> Pages -> Deploy from a branch -> \"\n                  \"gh-pages / root, then wait up to 10 minutes.\")\n        return result.returncode\n    return 1\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n\"\"\"T00: the walking skeleton. {"parentUuid":"c3e3be35-84f4-480f-a175-1486f40ba705","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWumJmr1xAaU5Gt9trEr","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've set `.claude/state/phase` to `spec` and read the repo. Before I write the spec, I need you to confirm the test seams, as the skill requires.\n\n**Proposed seams**\n\nThe template has two boundaries where the outside world touches the app, and both already exist. I'd test at both and nowhere lower:\n\n1. **The admin console over HTTP**, using the existing `client` and `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")` fixtures in `tests/conftest.py`.\n   - This covers login and logout, roles and Permission refusal, Post and Page create/edit/delete, slug rules, the Dashboard counts, the Content list filters, Preview, User management, Deactivated accounts and Password reset.\n   - Tests would send requests and check responses and redirects. They wouldn't query the database or call internal functions.\n2. **The published site on disk**, by calling `render_site(tmp_path)` against a seeded database and reading the files it writes.\n   - This covers:\n     - drafts never reaching `site/`\n     - sanitized Markdown\n     - Navigation built from published Pages\n     - Announcements order, First-published and Updated dates, and the five-item Home page\n     - Display name as the byline\n     - relative paths only\n     - copied image files\n   - Tests would read the HTML files. They wouldn't check the rendering code.\n\nThe ideal is one seam, but two is honest here. The console and the static export have no shared interface, and ADR-001 makes that split on purpose. Publishing is the rules' most important safety check (drafts, sanitizing, relative paths), so it deserves a direct test.\n\nThe first Admin bootstrap and `.env.example` reproducibility would be checked through the `cms` command, so they'd sit at the second seam.\n\n**Two things to know before I publish**\n\n- The tracker instructions ask for the `ready-for-agent` label, and the manual asks for a `spec` label. Neither exists in the repo yet (`gh label list` shows only GitHub's defaults). I'd create both, which is a small change to the repo's GitHub settings.\n- The spec is public, because the repo is public. It will use only what you told me and the brief, with no invented facts about ASA.\n\nDo these two seams match what you expect, and may I create the `spec` and `ready-for-agent` labels?"}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2094,"cache_read_input_tokens":91343,"output_tokens":933,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":192},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2094,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":933,"cache_read_input_tokens":91343,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2094,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2094},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":1,"requestId":"req_011CfWumJQXDVbxMDhNXXMKU","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:to-spec","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"b5d48a36-7dc0-4ad1-87bd-d58ede2bf777","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:04:42.839Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
