{"parentUuid":"af7e2f6b-a5c5-46fe-9d7d-738574f57dce","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvQapmg1qUxZyya8vov","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I've set `.claude/state/phase` to `tickets`. The manual asks for 7–9 tickets, and the first three must be deployable for the Stage 1 tag (`mp2-mvp`). Here is the proposed breakdown of 8, before I publish anything.\n\n**1. T01: Sign in, sign out and the first Admin**\n- **Blocked by:** none.\n- **Delivers:** `cms` creates the first Admin from an environment password. Admins and Editors sign in and out, and passwords are stored hashed. Deactivated accounts are refused. Admin addresses send a signed-out visitor to sign-in. Every form carries a CSRF token. The dashboard is only a placeholder.\n- **Demo:** run the setup from `.env.example`, sign in, sign out.\n\n**2. T02: An Editor writes a Post, with roles and Permission refusal**\n- **Blocked by:** T01.\n- **Delivers:**\n  - Create, edit and list Posts as Draft or Published.\n  - The slug is generated on first save, and numbered when it already exists.\n  - The Author is recorded, along with created, updated and First-published times.\n  - Post deletion and slug changes are Admin-only, with a warning on slug changes.\n  - The central permission rules are built here, and Editors get the \"You don't have permission\" page.\n- **Demo:** an Editor saves and publishes a Post, then is refused when they try to delete it.\n\n**3. T03: Published Posts go Live on the public site**\n- **Blocked by:** T02.\n- **Delivers:**\n  - `cms publish` writes the Home page and the Announcements list, ordered by First-published date.\n  - It writes one page per Published Post, with Markdown sanitized.\n  - Drafts never reach the export, and every link is relative.\n  - `cms deploy` sends it to GitHub Pages.\n- **Demo:** the live URL shows a Published Post and hides a Draft. This ticket completes the MVP.\n\n**4. T04: The Admin manages accounts**\n- **Blocked by:** T02.\n- **Delivers:**\n  - Create accounts with a role and a Display name, and change roles.\n  - Deactivate and reactivate accounts.\n  - Password reset by the Admin, never shown or logged.\n  - Display name changes.\n  - The byline uses the Display name, never the Username.\n  - Posts keep their Author after deactivation.\n  - Editors are refused on user management.\n\n**5. T05: Pages in the Navigation, with limited Editor rights**\n- **Blocked by:** T02, T03.\n- **Delivers:**\n  - The Admin creates, publishes, unpublishes, retitles, re-slugs and deletes Pages.\n  - Published Pages appear in the public Navigation.\n  - Editors can edit only the Body of an existing Page and are refused on everything else.\n\n**6. T06: Dashboard, Content list and Preview**\n- **Blocked by:** T05.\n- **Delivers:**\n  - The four content counts, plus account counts for the Admin only.\n  - The reminder that Published is not Live.\n  - One Content list filtered by Post/Page and Draft/Published.\n  - A Preview that matches the public render.\n  - The dismissible Event details hint.\n\n**7. T07: Polished, accessible public site**\n- **Blocked by:** T03, T05.\n- **Delivers:**\n  - Event cards with First-published and Updated dates.\n  - The Home page shows the Welcome text and five Announcements, with a link to all.\n  - A Photos page with images copied from the project into the site, alt text and captions.\n  - Demo illustrations labeled as illustrative.\n  - A phone-width layout, heading order, contrast and keyboard access.\n\n**8. T08: Demo-ready and documented**\n- **Blocked by:** T04, T06, T07.\n- **Delivers:**\n  - Rebuildable demo data with both roles and a deactivated account.\n  - The release checklist, including the limit that an Editor can't add an image alone.\n  - README sections for the live URL, running locally and the AI use statement.\n  - A clean-clone smoke test from `.env.example`.\n  - The six-screen screenshots.\n\n**Questions**\n- Does the granularity feel right? T02 is the fattest: it holds Posts and the whole permission foundation. The alternative is to move roles and the refusal page into T01, but T01 would then have nothing Admin-only to demonstrate.\n- Are the blocking edges right? T04 could start as soon as T02 lands, in parallel with T03.\n- Should any ticket be merged or split? For example, T06 could be split, or T08 folded into T07.\n\nI'll label the tickets `ticket` and `ready-for-agent`, and I'd create the `ticket` label first since it doesn't exist yet. Tell me what to change, or approve and I'll publish them in dependency order."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1819,"cache_read_input_tokens":106796,"output_tokens":1603,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":0},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1819,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":1603,"cache_read_input_tokens":106796,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1819,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1819},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"apiBlockIndex":0,"requestId":"req_011CfWvQaLGBMjBHJYcVGFmP","attributionSkill":"mattpocock-skills:to-tickets","attributionPlugin":"mattpocock-skills","type":"assistant","uuid":"641a010f-ee2c-4488-8eeb-4cfd748aaddd","timestamp":"2026-09-29T02:13:10.742Z","effort":"medium","perTurnEffort":"medium","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"7e27274c-20ee-4fb1-a02d-7e6cc1e59112","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RLGshmi9GFHHi91RGwFXnk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b01.md","content":"## What to build\n\nAn Admin or Editor can sign in and out of the admin console. The first Admin is created from the command line with a password read from the environment, and a reproducible demo Editor account exists too, so later tickets can demonstrate Editor behaviour before user management is built. Roles (Admin, Editor) and the active/Deactivated state exist on every account from the start.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] A `cms` command creates the first Admin account. The password comes from the environment and is never printed, logged or committed.\n- [ ] The demo seed creates one Admin and one Editor, both with a public Display name, with passwords from the environment. The same demo accounts are available to tests through the existing `client_as(\"admin\")` and `client_as(\"editor\")` fixtures, which must log in successfully (the fixture currently posts an email-style identifier; reconcile the sign-in identifier and the fixture on purpose).\n- [ ] Sign-in accepts a correct username/password and sends the user to the dashboard placeholder. A wrong password or unknown username shows one generic message that does not reveal which was wrong.\n- [ ] Passwords are stored hashed with argon2. No response, log or database row contains a plain password.\n- [ ] A Deactivated account cannot sign in, and an existing session for it stops working.\n- [ ] Sign-out ends the session.\n- [ ] Any admin address, visited while signed out, sends the visitor to sign-in.\n- [ ] Every state-changing form (including sign-in and sign-out) carries a CSRF token, and a request without a valid token is rejected.\n- [ ] `.env.example` lists every variable needed; `.env`, database files and secrets stay out of Git.\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover sign-in success and failure, Deactivated accounts, sign-out, redirects when signed out, and CSRF rejection. {"parentUuid":"428e3a3c-803a-47ee-a894-75b701bf72c2","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01VsANcs2Nxp9N1jtMzwYcSh","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b02.md","content":"## What to build\n\nAn Editor can write a Post, save it as a Draft, edit it, and mark it Published. The Post gets its slug automatically. The permission rules exist in one central place, and an Editor who attempts an Admin-only action (deleting a Post, changing a slug) sees the \"You don't have permission\" page. Demonstrated with the demo Editor and Admin from T01.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Editor can create a Post with a title and a Markdown Body, save it as a Draft, edit it later, and mark it Published or unpublish it. The Author is recorded automatically as the signed-in account. Created, updated and First-published times are recorded automatically.\n- [ ] The slug is generated from the title on first save, with a number added when it already exists. Editors never enter it. It does not change when the title or Body is later edited.\n- [ ] The First-published date is set the first time the Post becomes Published and is kept if the Post is unpublished and Published again.\n- [ ] An Editor can edit any Post, including one written by another account.\n- [ ] Only an Admin can delete a Post, and only an Admin can change a slug. The slug form warns that changing it can break an old link.\n- [ ] All permissions are checked by the server from one central rule table. Controls an Editor cannot use are hidden, but tests prove the server refuses the request when sent directly.\n- [ ] An Editor who tries an Admin-only action, by typed address or direct submission, sees a clear \"You don't have permission\" page with a link back to the dashboard. A signed-out visitor is sent to sign-in instead.\n- [ ] Every state-changing Post form carries a CSRF token.\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover: Draft/Published transitions; slug generation, numbering and stability; First-published date rules; each allowed and refused case in the Post part of the permission matrix; the refusal page; and CSRF.\n- [ ] The tests are read before they are allowed to pass, to catch a test written against a misunderstanding.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"output_tokens":7979,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":707},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":7979,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01VsANcs2Nxp9N1jtMzwYcSh":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b02.md","content":"## What to build\n\nAn Editor can write a Post, save it as a Draft, edit it, and mark it Published. {"parentUuid":"160cb0d1-4a0a-4b89-9183-ef0f5dd80b98","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Vsob1FM8T3CPDia95EjaFp","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b03.md","content":"## What to build\n\nPublished Posts appear on the public site. `cms publish` renders the Home page and the Announcements list (ordered by First-published date, newest first) plus one page per Published Post, and `cms deploy` sends it to GitHub Pages so the content is Live. Drafts never reach the export. This is the last ticket of the Stage 1 MVP: it must be deployed and checked at the live URL.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] `cms publish` writes a Home page, an Announcements page listing all Published Posts newest first by First-published date, and one page per Published Post.\n- [ ] Draft and unpublished Posts appear nowhere in the export: not in a list, not as a page, not in any text.\n- [ ] Post Bodies are rendered from Markdown and sanitized: raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms are stripped, while headings, emphasis, lists, links and images with alt text remain.\n- [ ] Every `href` and `src` in the export is relative. There is no root-absolute path.\n- [ ] Each Post shows its byline (the Author's Display name, never the Username) and its First-published date, which is not labelled as the event date.\n- [ ] Editing a Post after it is Published does not reorder the Announcements list.\n- [ ] `cms deploy` pushes the export to the GitHub Pages branch. The live URL shows a Published demo Post and does not show a Draft.\n- [ ] Tests at the published-site-on-disk seam cover: Drafts absent, sanitizing, ordering, byline, relative paths only, and stable order after an edit.\n- [ ] The Stage 1 checker passes and the `mp2-mvp` tag can be created after this ticket closes.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"output_tokens":7979,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":707},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":7979,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01Vsob1FM8T3CPDia95EjaFp":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b03.md","content":"## What to build\n\nPublished Posts appear on the public site. `cms publish` renders the Home page and the Announcements list (ordered by First-published date, newest first) plus one page per Published Post, and `cms deploy` sends it to GitHub Pages so the content is Live. This is the last ticket of the Stage 1 MVP: it must be deployed and checked at the live URL.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] `cms publish` writes a Home page, an Announcements page listing all Published Posts newest first by First-published date, and one page per Published Post.\n- [ ] Draft and unpublished Posts appear nowhere in the export: not in a list, not as a page, not in any text.\n- [ ] Post Bodies are rendered from Markdown and sanitized: raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms are stripped, while headings, emphasis, lists, links and images with alt text remain.\n- [ ] Every `href` and `src` in the export is relative. There is no root-absolute path.\n- [ ] Each Post shows its byline (the Author's Display name, never the Username) and its First-published date, which is not labelled as the event date.\n- [ ] Editing a Post after it is Published does not reorder the Announcements list.\n- [ ] `cms deploy` pushes the export to the GitHub Pages branch. The live URL shows a Published demo Post and does not show a Draft.\n- [ ] Tests at the published-site-on-disk seam cover: Drafts absent, sanitizing, ordering, byline, relative paths only, and stable order after an edit.\n- [ ] The Stage 1 checker passes and the `mp2-mvp` tag can be created after this ticket closes.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b8bb93ce-84e2-4bfa-a71b-0017094207b7","session_id":"35af8ee5-7e62-41b5-a09d-9972647e2fab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/yawoppong-krampah/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88","version":"2.1.284","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"ad9e6275-b1fa-4436-94e9-839a352c412d","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0196JFD2VxyydiKgQCSgxCMV","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b04.md","content":"## What to build\n\nThe Admin can manage accounts from the console: create them with a role and a public Display name, change roles, deactivate and reactivate them, reset a lost password, and change a Display name. A Post's public byline follows its Author's current Display name. Editors are refused on every user-management screen.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Admin can create an account with a private username, a password, a role (Admin or Editor) and a public Display name.\n- [ ] An Admin can change an account's role.\n- [ ] An Admin can deactivate an account, after which its owner cannot sign in and any existing session stops working. An Admin can reactivate it. Accounts are never deleted.\n- [ ] An Admin can set a new password for an existing account. The old password cannot be seen. The new one is stored hashed and is never printed or logged.\n- [ ] An Admin can change an account's Display name, for example to \"ASA Events Team\". Published Posts by that Author show the new Display name after the next publish.\n- [ ] The Author link on every Post is kept after deactivation or a Display name change, and Posts by a Deactivated account stay Published.\n- [ ] The private username is never used as a byline.\n- [ ] An Editor who visits user management, or submits any account action directly, sees the \"You don't have permission\" page. Tests prove refusal for every action, not just for hidden buttons.\n- [ ] Every account form carries a CSRF token.\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover account creation, role changes, deactivation and reactivation, password reset (including that the password does not appear in any response), Display name changes, and every Editor refusal. A Post's public byline follows its Author's current Display name. Editors are refused on every user-management screen.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Admin can create an account with a private username, a password, a role (Admin or Editor) and a public Display name.\n- [ ] An Admin can change an account's role.\n- [ ] An Admin can deactivate an account, after which its owner cannot sign in and any existing session stops working. An Admin can reactivate it. Accounts are never deleted.\n- [ ] An Admin can set a new password for an existing account. The old password cannot be seen. The new one is stored hashed and is never printed or logged.\n- [ ] An Admin can change an account's Display name, for example to \"ASA Events Team\". Published Posts by that Author show the new Display name after the next publish.\n- [ ] The Author link on every Post is kept after deactivation or a Display name change, and Posts by a Deactivated account stay Published.\n- [ ] The private username is never used as a byline.\n- [ ] An Editor who visits user management, or submits any account action directly, sees the \"You don't have permission\" page. Tests prove refusal for every action, not just for hidden buttons.\n- [ ] Every account form carries a CSRF token.\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover account creation, role changes, deactivation and reactivation, password reset (including that the password does not appear in any response), Display name changes, and every Editor refusal. {"parentUuid":"8f1258ff-f26c-4d4a-887f-250ac5e687f1","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0173Qi1h5vAsmkzgBVnQsPdF","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b05.md","content":"## What to build\n\nThe Admin can create and manage Pages, and Published Pages appear in the public Navigation. An Editor can update only the Body of an existing Page, so the Photos page can stay current, and is refused everything else on Pages. The Navigation lists Announcements plus every Published Page (demo starter Pages: About ASA, Resources, Photos).\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] An Admin can create a Page with a title, a slug and a Markdown Body, and can edit, publish, unpublish, retitle, re-slug and delete it.\n- [ ] A Published Page appears in the public Navigation, next to Announcements. An unpublished or Draft Page appears nowhere in the export.\n- [ ] An Editor can edit the Body of an existing Page. An Editor cannot create, delete, publish, unpublish, retitle or re-slug a Page, and is shown the \"You don't have permission\" page when they try, including by direct submission.\n- [ ] Controls an Editor cannot use are hidden on their screens, but the server refuses the request regardless.\n- [ ] Page Bodies use the same Markdown and sanitizing rules as Posts.\n- [ ] The Navigation and every Page link in the export use relative paths only.\n- [ ] Changing a Page's slug shows the same broken-link warning as for Posts.\n- [ ] The demo seed includes the starter Pages About ASA, Resources and Photos as clearly labelled placeholders, with no claims about ASA.\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover each Page permission row (allowed and refused) and CSRF. Tests at the published-site seam cover Navigation from Published Pages only, relative paths, and the absence of Draft Pages.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"output_tokens":7979,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":707},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":7979,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_0173Qi1h5vAsmkzgBVnQsPdF":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b05.md","content":"## What to build\n\nThe Admin can create and manage Pages, and Published Pages appear in the public Navigation. {"parentUuid":"ed8bfb25-5ea1-4a1b-a08f-b9d7a74f8d05","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_013pq5n2WZ4yHTRUrnmoTHEa","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b06.md","content":"## What to build\n\nAfter signing in, the user lands on a Dashboard showing the state of the site at a glance, and can find any Post or Page from one filterable Content list without opening each item. It stays simple on a phone.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] The Dashboard shows four counts to every signed-in user: Published Posts, Draft Posts, Published Pages, Draft Pages.\n- [ ] Only the Admin also sees the number of active accounts and the number of Deactivated accounts. An Editor's Dashboard never contains them, and the numbers are not reachable by a direct request.\n- [ ] The Dashboard shows a clear reminder that marking content Published does not make it Live until `cms publish` and `cms deploy` run. It has no \"Changes not live yet\" indicator.\n- [ ] One Content list shows both Posts and Pages and can be filtered by type (Post or Page) and by status (Draft or Published), separately and together.\n- [ ] Each row shows title, status, public Display name and last-updated time.\n- [ ] The Dashboard and Content list stay usable at a phone width of about 390 px.\n- [ ] Counts and list update immediately after content changes (create, publish, unpublish, delete).\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover counts for each role, the reminder text, each filter combination, and the row fields.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"output_tokens":7979,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":707},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":7979,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_013pq5n2WZ4yHTRUrnmoTHEa":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b06.md","content":"## What to build\n\nAfter signing in, the user lands on a Dashboard showing the state of the site at a glance, and can find any Post or Page from one filterable Content list without opening each item. {"parentUuid":"b78a53d0-dbf7-42fc-994e-67bee0d80c33","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EbdupUNNumD2SBw9MB4iBB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b07.md","content":"## What to build\n\nThe public site looks polished and welcoming to a first-time visitor and works on a phone. The Home page shows a short Welcome text and the five most recent Announcements as clear Event cards, with a link to all of them. Photos inside Posts and Pages appear with alt text. The site is accessible.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] The Home page shows the Welcome text, using only the client brief's wording (a student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community), followed by the five most recent Published Announcements and a link to the full Announcements page.\n- [ ] Each Announcement appears as an Event card showing its title, First-published date and byline. If the content changed after first publishing, the card also shows an \"Updated\" date. The First-published date is never labelled as the event date.\n- [ ] A Post that is unpublished and Published again keeps its original First-published date and position.\n- [ ] Approved image files added to the project by the release-holder are copied into the export by `cms publish`, and Markdown image references resolve with relative paths. An Editor writes only the caption, alt text and reference.\n- [ ] The Photos page shows placeholder or rights-cleared demo images labelled as illustrative, and says that actual event photos will be added when ASA has permission to use them. Nothing presents a stock photo or illustration as a real ASA event.\n- [ ] Accessibility: a sensible heading order on every page, readable colour contrast, visible focus and full keyboard access, a layout that works at about 390 px, and alt text on every image.\n- [ ] Only relative paths appear in the export, and Drafts never appear.\n- [ ] Tests at the published-site seam cover the five-item Home page, the link to all Announcements, cards with First-published and Updated dates, image copying with relative paths, alt text on every image, heading order, and the Welcome text. No claim about ASA beyond the brief appears anywhere in the export.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"output_tokens":7979,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":707},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":7979,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01EbdupUNNumD2SBw9MB4iBB":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b07.md","content":"## What to build\n\nThe public site looks polished and welcoming to a first-time visitor and works on a phone. The Home page shows a short Welcome text and the five most recent Announcements as clear Event cards, with a link to all of them. Photos inside Posts and Pages appear with alt text. The site is accessible.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n- [ ] The Home page shows the Welcome text, using only the client brief's wording (a student group primarily for African students and students with African backgrounds, welcoming anyone interested in African cultures and community), followed by the five most recent Published Announcements and a link to the full Announcements page.\n- [ ] Each Announcement appears as an Event card showing its title, First-published date and byline. If the content changed after first publishing, the card also shows an \"Updated\" date. The First-published date is never labelled as the event date.\n- [ ] A Post that is unpublished and Published again keeps its original First-published date and position.\n- [ ] Approved image files added to the project by the release-holder are copied into the export by `cms publish`, and Markdown image references resolve with relative paths. An Editor writes only the caption, alt text and reference.\n- [ ] The Photos page shows placeholder or rights-cleared demo images labelled as illustrative, and says that actual event photos will be added when ASA has permission to use them. Nothing presents a stock photo or illustration as a real ASA event.\n- [ ] Accessibility: a sensible heading order on every page, readable colour contrast, visible focus and full keyboard access, a layout that works at about 390 px, and alt text on every image.\n- [ ] Only relative paths appear in the export, and Drafts never appear.\n- [ ] Tests at the published-site seam cover the five-item Home page, the link to all Announcements, cards with First-published and Updated dates, image copying with relative paths, alt text on every image, heading order, and the Welcome text. {"parentUuid":"a91c3ed6-eead-4055-be6e-f8738ac07769","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CfWvaooA6tjafHHDnnNY2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PRF3rvCbCKBAfEWGTZ81cN","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b08.md","content":"## What to build\n\nEditors get a Preview and an Event details hint in the Post and Page editors, and the whole project is made demo-ready and documented. This is a testable vertical slice (route, template and tests) as well as the demo, screenshots and documentation tasks. It must not be completed as documentation alone.\n\n## Acceptance criteria\n\n**Preview and hint (route, template, tests)**\n\n- [ ] The Post editor and the Page editor each have a Preview that shows exactly the content the public page will render, using the same rendering and sanitizing as the export. Raw HTML, scripts, iframes and forms do not run in the Preview.\n- [ ] The Post editor shows a short Event details hint (What, When, Where, Who can attend). The hint is never pre-filled into the Body, and an Editor can hide it without deleting any content.\n- [ ] Tests at the admin HTTP seam cover: Preview output matches the published-site output for the same Body (including sanitizing and an image with alt text); the hint appears in the Post editor, is not in the Body, and hiding it leaves the Body unchanged; and an Editor's Preview cannot be used to reach an Admin-only action.\n\n**Demo-ready and documented**\n\n- [ ] The demo seed rebuilds cleanly from `.env.example`: both roles, Published and Draft Posts and Pages, and one Deactivated account. It contains no real personal data and no claims about ASA beyond the brief.\n- [ ] A clean-clone smoke test runs the setup from `.env.example` plus the documented steps and reaches a working sign-in. No password, `.env` or database file is committed.\n- [ ] A release checklist covers `cms publish`, `cms deploy`, the relative-path check, adding approved image files, confirming Photo permission before a real photo is added, and the limit that an Editor cannot add an image file alone. It says that for the class demo the project author runs it, and does not claim ASA has an existing owner for this step or an approval process.\n- [ ] The README has Live URL, Run locally and Generative AI Use Statement sections, with the Backends used table.\n- [ ] Screenshots of the six admin screens (sign-in, dashboard, Content list, editor, users, and the permission-refusal page) at 1280 px and 390 px are saved in the screenshots folder.\n- [ ] The Stage 2 submission checker passes.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"output_tokens":7979,"output_tokens_details":{"thinking_tokens":707},"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":7979,"cache_read_input_tokens":110218,"cache_creation_input_tokens":255,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":255},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"wireToolInputs":{"toolu_01PRF3rvCbCKBAfEWGTZ81cN":{"file_path":"/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-yawoppong-krampah-code-iphs400-mp2-cms/83a150db-6068-46d4-bcea-b074059d3d88/scratchpad/b08.md","content":"## What to build\n\nEditors get a Preview and an Event details hint in the Post and Page editors, and the whole project is made demo-ready and documented. T02 can demonstrate Editor behavior before T04 exists.\n- **T08:** it now holds Preview and the Event details hint. It is a testable slice with two parts.\n  - **Preview and hint:** the acceptance criteria cover route, template and tests. The Preview must match the public output and the hint must never be pre-filled into the Body.\n  - **Demo and release:** the same ticket holds the demo seed, the clean-clone smoke test, the release checklist, the README and the screenshots.\n- **T06** is now only the Dashboard counts and the filterable Content list.\n\n**Two things to watch**\n- **Two numbering schemes:** Ticket ids (T01–T08) and issue numbers (#2–#9) differ by one. Commits must start with the ticket id and end with the issue number. For T01 that is `T01: … Closes #2`, as `CLAUDE.md` requires.\n- **Fixture mismatch:** the existing `client_as` fixture in `tests/conftest.py` signs in with an email-style identifier, but the spec calls for a username. I put this in T01's acceptance criteria: reconcile the identifier and the fixture on purpose.\n\nI haven't started any ticket, and `.claude/state/phase` still says `tickets`.
